## Samiir el Volahrn

Samiir el Volahrn was a Calishite merchant, owner of the Pearled Harp trading coster, and pasha in the city of Calimport, Calimshan, in the 14 century DR.

Activities
He managed the Pearled Harp trading coster, also called the Purple Pearls, which traded in both Calimport and Cormyr. In this role, he was quite powerful.

Home
Samiir lived in the Pearls, a fine house in Azhaya Drudach, Marekh Sabban, in the city's Emerald Ward. The house reached three stories and had an outer walled yard.

Relationships
He had a daughter, Vanda Volahrn, with a courtesan in Suzail, Cormyr. She would be his sole surviving offspring and heir.This implies Samiir had other children who'd died. They may have been murdered, given how her father's rivals later target Vanda, but another possibility is the deadly Trades Conflagration of 1369 DR, which could've caused the whole family to perish. This supported by the wording "Vanda survived only as she was living... in Suzail."

History
When Samiir el Volahrn passed away in the late 1360s DR,This is "recently" as of Flamerule in 1370 DR. he had no heirs to his business. Thus, agents of Syl-Pasha Ralan el Pesarkhal went to Suzail to inform Vanda of her of her father's passing and of her birthright. They offered her Samiir's status as pasha and ownership of his trading coster, provided she used it to support certain trade alliances beneficial to Calimshan's ruler. This made her one of the only female pashas in Calimport and blocked rival merchants who'd coveted her father's coster for themselves, and a target for death.

## Saskia

Saskia was an Uthgardt sorceress and warrior of the Great Worm tribe turned mercenary in the Company of the Chimera in the late 14 century DR.

Description
The tall woman had snow-white skin, blue eyes with a crystalline gleam, and raven-black hair down to the middle of her back.

Personality
Saskia thought poorly of guard work, civilization, and people who weren't warriors. However, her scorn for them was weaker than her need for company.

Abilities
Besides being a competent brawler and sword-fighter, Saskia had sorcerous powers that she eventually embraced.

Possessions
The barbarian bore a bastard sword, and was known to wear heavy furs and a sealskin cloak.

Activities
She'd worked in the Company of the Chimera for perhaps a year.

Relationships
After being cast out from the Great Worm tribe, she became a mercenary for the Company of the Chimera, under the command of Tombli, whom she thought needed taking down a peg or two.

After fleeing the Company, she teamed up with the pseudodragon Aeristhax.

History
Saskia was ostracized from the Great Worm tribe for a number of suspicious coincidences that marked her as a witch, but which were signs of budding sorcery. She was offered an ultimatum: to either take the Trial of the Dragon, or leave. The trial called for her to meet with the tribe's spirit at the top of the Uthgardtheim called the Great Worm—actually a white dragon. Unfortunately, two days into her quest, an adventuring band had arrived ahead of her and slain the Great Worm, making it impossible for her to succeed. After eight days of fruitless waiting, she returned to her tribe with a fever; as soon as it broke, she learned of the Great Worm's fate and chose exile, leaving south.

There, she eventually joined with the Company of the Chimera in Sembia; this happened perhaps a year before Mirtul of the . On Mirtul 21 of that year, she clashed with Tombli, captain of the company, who was trying to order her to sit with the others and drink, or fight him. She chose to fight; though she briefly had the upper hand, he had his men pull her off of him. She sustained some wounds, which required treatment by Grummond, the Company's surgeon. Before they were done healing, she was called on by Tombli to identify a creature he was trying to buy. Saskia confirmed it was a pseudodragon, and it mind-spoke to her, asking to be freed. She refused in her heart, and got stung from inside the cage, which made her fall asleep for the rest of the day, having uneasy dreams about her past.

Upon awakening, she decided to free the pseudodragon. She ambushed Tombli, who was alone tormenting the caged creature in the stables at the time. He briefly overpowered her, but Saskia managed to employ some sorcery to free the dragon, which rescued her from the dwarf. She then fled the men from the Company that came after her, stealing a horse and reaching Tassledale by morning.

Saz-sass could call upon divine punishment, obscuring mist, searing light sound burst, and symbol of stunning.

History
During the Sschindylryn invasion of Eveningstar in the mid-1480s DR, Saz-sass and a number of other yuan-ti were hired by Sschindylryn to poison the water supply of Eveningstar. Along with Fless-onas and Gresl-glis, they secured a section of underground caverns that fed the groundwater and guarded the green dragon Tharmalos. Saz-sass was responsible for maintaining the last magical barrier before Tharmalos, and was slain while defending it.
